Despite its apparent simplicity, the dynamics remain poorly understood, in part because the loss landscape contains multiple non-strict saddle points, making it unclear why gradient descent from random initialization reliably escapes bad stationary regions. We provide a detailed characterization of the optimization landscape and prove that gradient descent from a moderately small random initialization-simultaneously training both layers-converges to a global minimizer at a linear rate with order-wise optimal sample complexity. Our analysis tracks the trajectory through three phases: an alignment phase in which hidden weights progressively align with the planted direction while the output weights maintain the correct sign pattern; a growth phase in which the norms of both layers increase while preserving alignment; and a local refinement phase in which the aligned neurons rapidly converge to the planted direction, yielding fast local convergence. To rigorously show that GD avoids non-strict saddles, we develop trajectory-level control arguments for the end-to-end dynamics. In addition, we establish novel uniform concentration results that hold along the entire trajectory, and are essential for obtaining order-wise optimal sample complexity. We corroborate our theory with extensive experiments across a range of configurations.

</details>


### [155] [LimiX-2M: Mitigating Low-Rank Collapse and Attention Bottlenecks in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04485)
*Yuanrui Wang,Xingxuan Zhang,Han Yu,Mingchao Ming,Gang Ren,Hao Yuan,Li Mao,Yunjia Zhang,Chun Yuan,Peng Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RaBEL（值感知令牌化）和S→N→F（重排序双向块）组成的LimiX-2M模型，通过紧凑局部RBF特征扩展和改进计算路由，以2M参数超越TabPFN-v2等更大模型，显著提升表格基础模型的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（TFMs）性能虽可媲美树集成方法，但计算效率低下：标准仿射标量令牌化仅通过一维通道注入特征值变化，特征ID/位置信号无法增加值自由度，导致早期层值敏感性弱且隐藏状态冗余。

Method: 提出统一的"令牌化-路由"框架：1) RaBEL：将每个标量扩展为紧凑局部RBF特征（可选指数门控），改善条件数和浅层有效秩；2) S→N→F重排序双向块：先聚合跨样本上下文，再进行特征混合，并使用注意力池化，使计算与读出对齐。

Result: LimiX-2M（2M参数）在广泛使用的表格基准上优于更大的TabPFN-v2和TabICL基线，同时降低训练和推理成本。

Conclusion: 值感知令牌化和读出对齐路由是改进TFMs精度-效率权衡的关键杠杆。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) increasingly rival tree ensembles, but their performance is often compute-inefficient: with standard affine scalar tokenization, each feature injects value variation through an essentially one-dimensional channel, and feature IDs/positional signals cannot increase within-feature value degrees of freedom, yielding weak early-layer value sensitivity and redundant hidden states. We present a unified \emph{tokenize-and-route} framework for strong TFMs: \textbf{RaBEL} expands each scalar into compact localized RBF features (optionally exponent-gated) to improve conditioning and shallow-layer effective rank, while a reordered bidirectional block \textbf{S$\rightarrow$N$\rightarrow$F} aligns computation with the readout by aggregating cross-sample context before feature mixing and using attention pooling. Together, these changes yield \textbf{LimiX-2M}, a 2M-parameter model that outperforms larger TabPFN-v2 and TabICL baselines on widely used tabular benchmarks while reducing training and inference costs. These results highlight value-aware tokenization and readout-aligned routing as key levers for improving the accuracy--efficiency trade-off in TFMs. Model checkpoints and inference code are available at https://github.com/limix-ldm-ai/LimiX.

</details>


### [156] [Episodic Memory Temporal Consistency for Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04492)
*Zicheng Zhao,Yu Lan,Chengzhengxu Li,Zhaohan Zhang,Xiaoming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMTC框架，通过时序一致性语义嵌入器（对比学习+时间条件状态重建）和时序一致性门控机制解决多智能体强化学习中的奖励稀疏和探索瓶颈问题，在SMAC和GRF基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体强化学习面临严重的奖励稀疏性和探索瓶颈，现有情节记忆机制虽然能复用高回报轨迹，但因无约束的激励分配和语义表征坍缩，容易陷入局部最优。

Method: 提出EMTC框架，包含两个协同组件：(1) 时序一致性语义嵌入器，结合对比学习与时间条件状态重建，防止表征坍缩并实现精确记忆检索；(2) 时序一致性门控机制，基于时序一致性误差动态调节情节激励，过滤伪成功轨迹的误导信号，缓解Q值过估计。

Result: 在SMAC和GRF基准上全面超越SOTA基线；在SMAC超难场景中相比最强情节基线绝对胜率提升24%，在GRF任务中平均提升28%。

Conclusion: EMTC通过时序一致性约束有效解决了情节记忆中的表征坍缩和激励分配问题，理论证明误差界限与轨迹最优性和表征质量直接关联，实践经验证具有显著优势。

Abstract: Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) frequently suffers from severe reward sparsity and exploration bottlenecks. While episodic memory mechanisms mitigate these issues by reusing high-return trajectories, they often trap agents in local optima due to unconstrained incentive distribution and semantic representation collapse. To address this, we propose Episodic Memory Temporal Consistency (EMTC), a framework that robustly constructs and selectively leverages historical experiences. EMTC introduces two synergistic components: (1) a Temporally Consistent Semantic Embedder that integrates contrastive learning with time-conditioned state reconstruction, preventing representation collapse and enabling precise memory retrieval; and (2) a Temporal Consistency Gating Mechanism that dynamically modulates episodic incentives based on temporal consistency error. This adaptive gate filters misleading signals from pseudo-successful trajectories, effectively mitigating Q-value overestimation. We provide theoretical guarantees, establishing a strict error bound that directly links the observable temporal consistency error to the underlying trajectory optimality and representation quality. Extensive evaluations on the SMAC and GRF benchmarks demonstrate that EMTC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Notably, compared to the strongest episodic baseline, EMTC achieves absolute win-rate improvements of up to 24% in super-hard SMAC scenarios and an average improvement of 28% across GRF tasks.

</details>


### [157] [Smart Picks in the Dark: Towards Efficient RLVR for Reasoning via Tracing Metacognitive Pivots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04503)
*Guangcheng Zhu,Shenzhi Yang,Haobo Wang,Xing Zheng,Yingfan MA,Xuening Feng,Zhongqi Chen,Bowen Song,Weiqiang Wang,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PivotTrace框架，通过注意力动态追踪推理中的元认知转折点来量化不确定性，在无需预先标注的“暗中选择”设置下，仅用29.3%的标注样本和2.75倍更快的收敛速度即超越全监督的大推理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可验证奖励强化学习(RLVR)方法面临数据效率瓶颈：有监督数据选择方法依赖已标注数据池，无监督RLVR方法性能欠佳。作者希望在无先验监督的条件下，从海量未标注数据中选出最有价值的样本进行标注和训练，即"暗中选择"设置。

Method: 提出PivotTrace三路数据分流框架，利用注意力动态追踪推理过程中的元认知转折点(pivots)，通过转折点密度精确量化不确定性，实现数据的自动化路由，协同最大化标注与训练效率。

Result: 仅用29.3%的标注样本和2.75倍更快的收敛速度，即超越了全监督的大推理模型性能。

Conclusion: 智能的数据选择依赖于良好校准的不确定性估计器，通过转折点密度量化不确定性可实现高效的数据分流，显著提升RLVR的数据与训练效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has greatly advanced large reasoning models (LRMs), but it requires timely training on a huge fully-annotated dataset. To this end, data-efficient RLVR methods have been widely studied from two perspectives: (i) data selection methods identify a small subset of "golden" samples that yield near-full-data performance, but they rely on a pre-existing pool of labeled data. (ii) unsupervised RLVR methods train the model using its own internal supervision signals on large-scale unlabeled data, yet they exhibit suboptimal performance. Accordingly, we investigate the "pick in the dark" setup for RLVR, which aims to select, without prior supervision, unlabeled samples that are most beneficial for training and worthy of annotation. Through systematic analysis, we demonstrate that smart picks hinge on a well-calibrated uncertainty estimator to enable strategic partitioning of data for adaptive training regimes. Building on this insight, we propose PivotTrace, a three-way data triage framework that leverages attention dynamics to trace metacognitive pivots during reasoning. By precisely quantifying uncertainty through pivot density, PivotTrace achieves automated data routing to synergistically maximize both annotation and training efficiency. Empirically, PivotTrace surpasses the fully supervised LRM with only 29.3% annotated samples and 2.75 faster convergence.

</details>


### [158] [GeoMin: Data-Efficient Semi-Supervised RLVR via Geometric Distribution Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04516)
*Guangcheng Zhu,Shenzhi Yang,Haobo Wang,Xing Zheng,Yingfan MA,Xuening Feng,Zhongqi Chen,Kai Tang,Zhengqing Zang,Bowen Song,Weiqiang Wang,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoMin通过建模全局特征分布来解码正确与错误轨迹之间的结构差异，从而建立稳健先验以评估自奖励信号可靠性，在半监督RLVR中实现高效数据利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督RLVR方法因依赖粗粒度性能启发式而遭受严重的数据效率瓶颈，大量有价值的未标注实例未被充分利用。

Method: GeoMin在标注数据上建模全局特征分布，解码正确与错误rollout之间的结构差异，建立稳健先验来评估自奖励信号的可靠性，从而充分释放未标注数据的潜力。

Result: GeoMin在最强基线上提升+4.1%，且仅需10%标注量即可超越全监督模型，展现出卓越的数据效率。

Conclusion: GeoMin通过全局特征分布建模有效解决了半监督RLVR中的数据效率瓶颈，以极低标注成本实现了超越全监督的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) significantly advances LLM reasoning, yet it faces a dilemma: standard supervised scaling is throttled by high annotation costs, while unsupervised alternatives suffer from severe model collapse. Recent semi-supervised RLVR methods address this by using a small labeled set to guide unlabeled data, achieving a promising trade-off between training efficacy and annotation cost. However, they suffer from a severe data-efficiency bottleneck due to the reliance on coarse performance heuristics, leaving a vast majority of valuable instances underutilized. To this end, we propose GeoMin, which models global feature distributions on labeled data to decode the structural discrepancy between correct and incorrect rollouts, thereby establishing a robust prior to assess the reliability of self-reward signals and fully unleash the potential of unlabeled data. Empirically, GeoMin outperforms the strongest baselines by +4.1% and even surpasses fully supervised models with only 10% of the annotations, demonstrating remarkable data efficiency.

</details>


### [159] [Rollout-Level Advantage-Prioritized Experience Replay for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04560)
*Gyeongtae Yoo,Sanghyeok Park,Soohyuk Jang,Ik-hwan Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于轨迹级重放缓冲区的GRPO改进方法，通过年龄驱逐机制控制数据陈旧性、新鲜锚定组合保证策略一致性、以及基于优势值的优先级重放，显著提升了推理LLMs后训练的样本效率，在Qwen3-Base模型上最高取得4.35个百分点的平均准确率提升和0.579的AES效率增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO方法在LLM推理后训练中存在样本效率低下的问题：每次rollout仅用于一次梯度更新即被丢弃。简单的经验重放不适用，因为LLM策略每步梯度更新后漂移严重，存储的rollouts迅速过时，反而会破坏训练稳定性。

Method: 提出轨迹级重放缓冲区：1）年龄驱逐机制：超过τ_max训练步数的rollouts被移除，控制数据陈旧性；2）新鲜锚定组合：每个batch保留当前新鲜on-policy rollouts，再从缓冲区中单独抽取重放rollouts拼接；3）基于per-rollout优势值的优先级重放，对优势较大的rollouts进行回收重用。

Result: 在Qwen3-Base三个规模尺度、五个数学基准上，该方法持续优于GRPO和朴素重放基线。增益随模型规模增长，4B规模下五基准平均提升+4.35个百分点；AES效率指标上，4B规模下相较于GRPO提升+0.579。

Conclusion: 轨迹级重放缓冲区能有效提升GRPO的样本效率，且随模型规模扩大增益更加显著，验证了在策略漂移显著的LLM强化学习场景中，精心设计的重放机制比朴素重放更有效。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards with GRPO is a standard approach for post-training reasoning LLMs. It remains sample inefficient. Each rollout is used for a single gradient update and then discarded. Naive replay is not well suited in this setting because LLM policies drift quickly per gradient step. Stored rollouts therefore become stale and can destabilize training. We propose a rollout-level replay buffer for GRPO that stores and samples individual rollouts rather than whole groups. The buffer bounds staleness through age eviction. Any rollout older than tau_max training steps is removed. The buffer also preserves on-policy data via fresh-anchored composition. Each batch keeps its fresh on-policy rollouts and then concatenates replay rollouts drawn separately from the buffer. We prioritize replay by per-rollout advantage magnitude and recycle individual rollouts whose advantages are large. Across three Qwen3-Base scales on five math benchmarks, our method outperforms GRPO and naive replay baselines. Gains are positive at every scale and grow with model size. The largest gain is +4.35 pp on the five-benchmark average at 4B. Under an AES metric that jointly measures accuracy and token efficiency, the efficiency margin over GRPO is again largest at 4B, at +0.579.

</details>


### [160] [SurvPFN: Towards Foundation Models for Survival Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04564)
*Samuel Böhm,Lennart Purucker,Frank Hutter,Pascal Schlosser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SurvPFN，一种基于先验数据拟合网络(PFN)的生存预测模型。通过在数百万合成生存任务上预训练，利用Weibull分布和删失负对数似然进行分布回归，无需逐数据集拟合或特征工程，即可在真实生存任务上达到与经典及深度生存基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准表格基础模型(TFM)无法处理删失数据，导致预测存在偏差和不准确，不适合实际生存分析应用。现有生存预测方法需要专用架构或逐数据集拟合，缺乏通用的预训练基础模型。

Method: (1) 使用Weibull分布生成事件时间，并引入非信息性删失机制；(2) 整合删失事件指示器；(3) 最小化删失负对数似然损失。在数百万合成生存任务上预训练，将生存预测建模为连续时间分布回归问题。

Result: 在SurvSet真实生存任务集合上，SurvPFN与经典生存模型（如Cox比例风险模型）和深度生存基线（如DeepSurv）高度竞争，且无需逐数据集拟合、专用生存架构或特征工程。

Conclusion: 生存预测可以视为带有删失损失的连续时间分布回归问题，这解锁了PFN在时间-事件预测任务上的潜力，为生存分析提供了一种通用的预训练基础模型范式。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have made rapid progress in standard classification and regression, but time-to-event survival prediction tasks have remained largely untouched. Unlike in standard regression tasks, survival prediction models must account for censored data. Standard TFMs cannot handle natively censored data, leading to biased and inaccurate predictions, making them unsuitable for real-world applications. To overcome this fundamental limitation, we propose \texttt{SurvPFN}, a prior-data fitted network (PFN), for survival prediction tasks. We pretrain \texttt{SurvPFN} on millions of synthetic survival prediction tasks to learn survival via distributional regression that accounts for censored data. \texttt{SurvPFN} works by (1) generating data with Weibull event times and a non-informative censoring mechanism; (2) integrating a censored event indicator; and (3) minimizing a censored negative log-likelihood. On SurvSet, a collection of real-world survival tasks, \texttt{SurvPFN} is highly competitive with classical and deep survival baselines without per-dataset fitting, a survival-specific architecture, or feature engineering. We show that survival can be treated as a continuous-time distributional regression problem with censored loss, unlocking the power of PFNs for time-to-event predictions.

</details>


### [161] [Dynamic Multi-Pair Trading Strategy in Cryptocurrency Markets with Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04574)
*Damian Lebiedź,Robert Ślepaczuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种将深度强化学习（DRL）作为执行覆盖层应用于加密货币市场配对交易的方法，通过分层"筛选-排序"配对选择方法和"固定风险、自适应均值"执行模型构建稳健系统。基于PPO+LSTM架构的策略在币安USD-M期货市场1小时间隔数据上表现优于启发式基线，并通过平稳圆形块自助法验证了风险调整后超额收益在10%水平上统计显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统配对交易策略在传统股票市场表现良好，但在高波动性加密货币市场中存在刚性和严重发散风险，亟需一种能够适应高方差环境的稳健执行方案。

Method: 采用分层"Filter-then-Rank"配对选择方法、专有的"Fixed Risk, Adaptive Mean"执行模型，以及带有LSTM层的PPO智能体，在严格的确定性风险管理边界内控制执行决策。

Result: 在币安USD-M期货市场1小时间隔数据上，优化后的RL策略在样本外表现显著优于启发式基线。平稳圆形块自助法检验证实，风险调整后的超额收益在10%显著性水平上统计显著，但未达到5%的严格阈值。

Conclusion: 本文为量化金融文献贡献了一种将统计套利与DRL执行策略相结合的混合架构，同时通过确定性屏蔽提供了一种安全强化学习的新框架，证明将神经策略锚定在统计稳健的边界上能有效缓解严重发散风险。

Abstract: This study aims to determine whether the application of Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) as a specialized execution overlay can enhance pair trading in highly volatile cryptocurrency markets. Although classical implementations of the strategy have proven successful in traditional equities, they frequently exhibit rigidity and suffer from severe divergence risks when applied to high-variance environments. To address this need, this research introduces novel concepts. To construct a robust system, we developed a hierarchical "Filter-then-Rank" pair selection methodology and a proprietary "Fixed Risk, Adaptive Mean" execution model. The system employs a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) agent with a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) layer to govern execution decisions within strict deterministic risk management boundaries. Evaluated on 1-hour interval data from the Binance USD-M Futures market, the optimized RL policy achieved an out-of-sample performance that substantially outperformed the heuristic baseline. A stationary circular block bootstrap robustness check confirms that the agent's risk-adjusted outperformance is statistically significant at the 10 percent level. Although falling marginally short of the stricter 5 percent threshold, this result highlights the extreme idiosyncratic variance characteristic of digital assets. Ultimately, this thesis contributes to the quantitative finance literature by introducing a hybrid architecture that combines statistical arbitrage with DRL execution policies. Furthermore, it delivers a novel framework for safe reinforcement learning via deterministic shielding, proving that anchoring a neural policy to statistically robust boundaries successfully mitigates severe divergence risks.

</details>


### [162] [HalfNet: Randomized Neural Networks with Learned Subspace Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04583)
*Ethem Alpaydin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HalfNet通过仅学习权重分布协方差矩阵的低秩分解（而非权重具体值），在MNIST和CIFAR-10上用更少参数达到全训练MLP的性能，揭示了神经网络的预测能力主要源于权重空间几何结构而非参数精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索神经网络中固定随机权重（仅学习分布几何结构）能否在显著减少参数的情况下匹配全训练网络的性能，并揭示网络预测能力的本质来源。

Method: 提出HalfNet，权重从N(0, Σ)随机采样，其中协方差矩阵Σ具有从数据中学习的低秩分解；在MNIST和CIFAR-10上进行多层感知机实验验证。

Result: HalfNet用更少参数匹配全训练MLP的性能；精度随秩平滑扩展；谱分析表明预测能力主要来自权重空间几何结构而非精确参数值。

Conclusion: 神经网络预测能力的核心在于权重空间的几何结构（分布协方差），而非单个权重的精确值；HalfNet可解释为数据依赖的随机嵌入、监督度量学习或随机特征/核方法。

Abstract: Many researchers investigated neural networks with some of their weights fixed to values randomly drawn from a given distribution, e.g., $N(0, I)$. Our proposed HalfNet draws random weights from $N(0, Σ)$, where $Σ$, which defines the geometry of the distribution, has a low-rank factorization that we learn from data. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 demonstrate that HalfNet can match the performance of fully trained multilayer perceptrons while using substantially fewer parameters. Spectral analysis indicates that much of the predictive power of neural networks lies in the geometry of their weight space rather than in the precise values of individual parameters, and we observe that accuracy scales smoothly with rank. HalfNet is not a neural architecture trick for low-rank structure; it implements a data-dependent random embedding that can also be interpreted through supervised metric learning, or random-feature and kernel perspectives.

</details>


### [163] [QuBLAST: A Framework for Quantizing Large Language Models with Block-Level Compression Approach and Activation Scaling Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04620)
*Pasindu Wickramasinghe,Achyuta Muthuvelan,Rachmad Vidya Wicaksana Putra,Minghao Shao,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuBLAST是一种面向大型语言模型的后训练量化方法，通过块级混合精度量化和激活缩放策略，在减少模型大小40%-45.2%的同时保持困惑度增加不超过5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM量化方法存在三个局限：1) 对所有注意力块采用统一的量化级别，忽略了同一网络中不同块可采用不同量化精度的潜力；2) 使用复杂操作缓解激活异常值的影响，导致计算开销高；3) 未考虑采用非传统注意力架构（如状态空间模型）的新兴LLM，这些模型在量化时面临不同挑战。

Method: QuBLAST包含两个核心组件：1) 块级压缩方法——通过交叉熵损失分析评估预训练模型中不同注意力块的敏感性，据此为每个注意力块确定权重量化级别，实现混合精度量化；2) 激活缩放策略——为每个块设计激活缩放图，控制激活值范围，有效缓解激活异常值的负面影响。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B、Llama3-8B、Mistral v0.1-8B和Falcon H1R-7B等多种模型架构上，QuBLAST将模型大小减少40%-45.2%，同时在WikiText-2和WikiText-103数据集上困惑度增幅不超过5%。

Conclusion: QuBLAST通过块级混合精度量化和高效的激活缩放策略，在显著压缩LLM模型的同时较好地保持了性能，为嵌入式系统部署LLM提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLMs have become the state-of-the-art algorithms for solving NLP tasks. However, they typically come at huge computational and memory costs, thus making them difficult to deploy on embedded systems. Toward this, state-of-the-art methods typically employ uniform post-training quantization (PTQ) across attention blocks of the network, hence overlooking the potential of applying different quantization levels in the same network. They also employ complex operations to mitigate the negative impact of activation outliers, hence incurring high computational overheads. Moreover, they have not considered evaluation using emerging LLMs with non-conventional attention architectures (e.g., state-space models), which pose different challenges in applying quantization. To address these limitations, we propose QuBLAST, a novel PTQ methodology that employs block-level compression approach with activation scaling strategy for LLMs. Block-level compression approach enables mixed-precision quantization across blocks of the network, while activation scaling strategy efficiently mitigates the negative impact of activation outliers. Specifically, QuBLAST first analyzes the sensitivity of different attention blocks in the pre-trained model through the cross-entropy loss analysis. QuBLAST leverages this sensitivity analysis to determine the weight quantization level for each attention block in the model. Furthermore, QuBLAST employs the activation scaling map for each block to control the range of activation values and mitigate the negative impact of activation outliers, thereby enabling better quantization results. Experimental results show that, QuBLAST reduces model sizes by 40%-45.2% across different model architectures (i.e., Qwen3-8B, Llama3-8B, Mistral v0.1-8B, and Falcon H1R-7B), while maintaining the performance within 5% perplexity increase for the WikiText-2 and WikiText-103 datasets.

</details>


### [164] [Learning symplectic model reduction based on a approximation theorem of symplectic embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04623)
*Liyi Feng,Yifa Tang,Yulin Xie,Ruili Zhang,Aiqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对高维哈密顿系统的模型降维问题，提出了辛保持自编码器（SpAE），通过将解码器参数化为辛嵌入、编码器构造为辛投影，在严格保持辛结构的同时实现高精度降维，并在高维格点和粒子系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维哈密顿系统在科学与工程中至关重要，但其动力学在辛流形上演化。标准自编码器在进行模型降维时容易破坏固有的辛结构，导致潜在坐标无法支持哈密顿流动，进而产生不稳定的长期预测。

Method: 首先建立了辛嵌入的通用逼近定理；基于该理论，提出辛保持自编码器（SpAE），其中解码器参数化为辛嵌入，编码器构造为对应的辛投影。该架构在结构上精确保持辛结构，可通过标准无约束优化训练。

Result: 在高维格点和粒子系统上的大量实验表明，所提方法在重构精度和预测精度上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: SpAE架构表达能力足以逼近非线性辛嵌入及相应的辛投影，能够严格保持辛结构，有效提升降维模型的长期预测稳定性。

Abstract: High-dimensional Hamiltonian systems play a central role in many scientific and engineering disciplines, with dynamics evolving on symplectic manifolds. Although deep learning provides powerful tools for constructing low-dimensional surrogates from data, the intrinsic symplectic structure is easily destroyed during model reduction. As a result, a standard autoencoder may produce latent coordinates that do not support a Hamiltonian flow, leading to unstable long-time prediction. In this paper, we first establish a universal approximation theorem for symplectic embeddings. Based on this theory, we propose symplecticity-preserving autoencoders (SpAE), in which the decoder is parameterized as a symplectic embedding and the encoder is constructed as the corresponding symplectic projection. This architecture is expressive enough to approximate nonlinear symplectic embeddings and the associated symplectic projections, preserves the symplectic structure exactly by construction, and can be trained by standard unconstrained optimization, thereby improving both reconstruction and prediction accuracy. Extensive experiments on high-dimensional lattice and particle systems demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [165] [VentAgent: When LLMs Learn to Breathe -- Multi-Objective Arbitration for ARDS Ventilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04632)
*Teqi Hao,Yuxuan Fu,Xiaoyu Tan,Shaojie Shi,Bohao Lv,Yinghui Xu,Xihe Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VentAgent是一个基于大语言模型的分层框架，将机械通气控制重构为动态多目标仲裁过程，通过可解释的三阶段决策（感知-规划-编排）平衡ARDS治疗中的竞争性临床目标，在高保真生理模拟器上优于现有强化学习和经典控制方法，并提供人类可读的推理链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法存在模仿偏差，无法泛化到波动或分布外的表型；标准强化学习方法难以处理重症监护中的对抗性权衡，且策略不透明缺乏临床可解释性。

Method: 提出VentAgent分层框架，将通气控制重构为动态多目标仲裁过程，包含三个可解释阶段：感知（Perception）、规划（Planning）和编排（Orchestration），利用LLM的语义推理能力综合异构专家策略，通过显式协调机制解决冲突的临床优先级。

Result: 在高保真生理模拟器上，VentAgent在性能上优于最先进的强化学习和经典控制基线方法。

Conclusion: VentAgent将控制决策转化为人类可读的推理链，为重症监护自动化提供了更安全、更具可解释性和适应性的范式。

Abstract: Mechanical ventilation for Acute Respiratory Distress Syndrome (ARDS) requires balancing competing physiological goals, including oxygenation, lung protection, and acid-base homeostasis. However, current data-driven methods, especially those imitating retrospective Electronic Health Records (EHR), often suffer from imitation bias. They may capture superficial correlations from inconsistent clinical demonstrations, such as associating passive ventilator settings with survival because such settings are common in stable patients, and thus fail to generalize to volatile or out-of-distribution phenotypes. Standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) methods also struggle with the adversarial trade-offs of critical care and often produce opaque policies with limited clinical interpretability. To address these limitations, we introduce VentAgent, a hierarchical framework in which Large Language Models (LLMs) act as transparent arbitrators for mechanical ventilation. We reformulate ventilation control as a dynamic Multi-Objective Arbitration process rather than single-objective optimization. VentAgent decomposes decision-making into three interpretable stages: Perception, Planning, and Orchestration. By leveraging the semantic reasoning capabilities of LLMs, it synthesizes strategies from heterogeneous experts and resolves conflicting clinical priorities through an explicit coordination mechanism. Evaluations on a high-fidelity physiological simulator show that VentAgent outperforms state-of-the-art RL and classical control baselines. Moreover, it converts control decisions into human-readable reasoning chains, offering a safer, more interpretable, and adaptable paradigm for critical care automation.

</details>


### [166] [Explainably Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04634)
*Sabine Rieder,Stefan Pranger,Debraj Chakraborty,Jan Křetínský,Bettina Könighofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层决策树方法，用于解释强化学习中屏蔽机制（Shield）的安全决策。该方法通过世界模型进行状态风险分类（安全、关键、危险、不安全），生成高层决策树解释风险原因，并在运行时生成局部决策树解释动作允许/禁止的理由。相比原始屏蔽器，决策树规模缩小数个数量级，且无需额外信息即可集成到现有屏蔽RL流程中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强对强化学习系统中屏蔽机制决策的可解释性，解决屏蔽器虽能保证安全但其决策过程（因非确定性导致决策树过大）难以被人类理解的问题，从而提升对安全关键决策系统的信任。

Method: 1）设计时：利用世界模型分析状态-动作的安全风险，据此构建屏蔽器和一个高层决策树，将状态分为安全、关键、危险、不安全四类风险类别。2）运行时：生成局部决策树，解释哪些动作被允许、哪些被禁止及其原因。采用分层决策树结构，提供自上而下的基于案例的解释。

Result: 生成的解释用决策树比原始屏蔽器小数个数量级，解释可理解性显著提升；方法开销极小，能无缝集成到现有屏蔽RL流水线中。

Conclusion: 分层决策树方法能够在不增加额外信息或显著开销的前提下，为安全屏蔽强化学习提供高效且人类可理解的解释，增强了系统透明度和用户信任。

Abstract: Trust in a decision-making system requires both safety guarantees and the ability to interpret and understand its behavior. This is particularly important for learned systems, whose decision-making processes are often highly opaque. Shielding is a prominent model-based technique for enforcing safety in reinforcement learning. However, because shields are automatically synthesized using rigorous formal methods, their decisions are often similarly difficult for humans to interpret. Recently, decision trees became customary to represent controllers and policies. However, since shields are inherently non-deterministic, their decision tree representations become too large to be explainable in practice. To address this challenge, we propose a novel approach for explainable safe RL that enhances trust by providing human-interpretable explanations of the shield's decisions. Our method represents the shielding policy as a hierarchy of decision trees, offering top-down, case-based explanations. At design time, we use a world model to analyze the safety risks of executing actions in given states. Based on this analysis, we construct both the shield and a high-level decision tree that classifies states into risk categories (safe, critical, dangerous, unsafe), explaining why a situation may be safety-critical. At runtime, we generate localized decision trees that explain which actions are allowed and why others are deemed unsafe. Our method facilitates explainability of the safety aspect in safe-by-shielding reinforcement learning, requires no additional information beyond what is already used for shielding, incurs minimal overhead, and integrates readily into existing shielded RL pipelines. In our experiments, we compute explanations using decision trees that are several orders of magnitude smaller than the original shield.

</details>


### [167] [ALINC: Active Learning for Inductive Node Classification via Graph Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04647)
*Pascal Plettenberg,Denis Huseljic,André Alcalde,Bernhard Sick,Josephine M. Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ALINC是一个针对归纳式节点分类的主动学习框架，通过图采样策略选择整图而非单节点进行标注，填补了现有方法在节点级效用提升到图级选择标准方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习方法主要针对单图或少数大图中的节点选择进行标注，但在分子化学、电子设计自动化等领域，数据集包含成千上万的独立图，且标注单个节点需要全图分析，从而自动获得其余节点标签。现有文献缺乏针对这类场景的整图选择策略。

Method: 提出ALINC框架，通过多种聚合机制将节点级效用度量提升为图级选择标准，从而实现图采样策略。在10种策略、3种聚合方法和4个数据集上进行了广泛基准测试。

Result: 识CoreSet、TypiClust和BADGE为最优图采样策略；聚合方法的选择显著影响模型性能和标注成本；在两个用例研究中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: ALINC有效填补了归纳式节点分类中主动学习图级选择的空白，聚合方法的选择是关键因素。

Abstract: Active learning (AL) for node classification typically focuses on selecting the most informative nodes for annotation within one or a few large graphs (e.g., in social network analysis). However, in other domains, such as molecular chemistry or electronic design automation, datasets consist of thousands of independent graphs. In many of these inductive settings, annotating an individual node requires a full-graph analysis, which effectively yields the remaining node labels on-the-fly. Therefore, these scenarios require AL strategies that select entire graphs instead of single nodes, a problem which has not been tackled in the literature so far. Thus, we introduce ALINC, an AL framework for inductive node classification via graph sampling. It bridges the existing methodological gap by elevating node-level utility measures to graph-level selection criteria through various aggregation mechanisms. In an extensive benchmark including ten strategies, three aggregation methods, and four datasets, we identify CoreSet, TypiClust, and BADGE as the top-performing graph sampling strategies. Our detailed analysis further reveals that the choice of the aggregation method is pivotal, as it substantially affects model performance and annotation costs. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of ALINC in two use case studies: site-of-metabolism prediction in molecules and design automation of printed circuit board schematics.

</details>


### [168] [Why Muon Outperforms Adam: A Curvature Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04662)
*Shuche Wang,Fengzhuo Zhang,Jiaxiang Li,Dirk Bergemann,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从曲率视角出发，揭示了Muon优化器在大型语言模型训练中相较于Adam效率提升约两倍的原因：Muon通过降低归一化方向锐度（NDS）从而获得更小的二阶曲率惩罚，且这一优势在数据不平衡和跨层曲率异质性条件下更为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在大语言模型训练中相较于Adam效率提升约两倍，但其局部几何来源尚不明确。本文旨在从曲率视角首次解释Muon优于Adam的原因。

Method: 1) 对训练损失进行二阶泰勒展开，比较Muon与Adam的一步损失下降量；2) 将曲率惩罚分解为更新范数平方与归一化方向锐度（NDS）；3) 使用Zipf-PCFG数据控制数据不平衡程度，分析其对NDS优势的影响；4) 进行层内/跨层分解，分析训练中后期NDS来源；5) 在具有异质性曲率和梯度对齐的二次问题上进行理论证明。

Result: Muon在相同验证损失下比Adam取得更大的一步损失下降；两者一阶增益相当，但Muon的二阶曲率惩罚更小；曲率惩罚差异主要由更低的NDS驱动而非更新范数；数据不平衡放大Muon的NDS优势；训练中后期Muon的NDS优势主要来自更小的层内曲率；理论证明Muon通过平衡不同曲率组间的更新能量实现更小的平均NDS。

Conclusion: Muon的优势源于其能更有效地降低归一化方向锐度，从而减轻二阶曲率惩罚，尤其在数据不平衡和曲率异质性强的场景下更为显著。

Abstract: Muon improves training efficiency over Adam in large language-model training by about two times, but the local geometric source of this advantage remains unclear. Our work takes a first step toward demystifying Muon's superiority over Adam from a curvature perspective. First, we apply a second-order Taylor approximation to the training landscape and show that Muon achieves a larger one-step loss decrease than Adam at matched validation loss. The two optimizers have comparable first-order gains, but Muon consistently incurs a smaller second-order curvature penalty. Second, we decompose this curvature penalty into the squared update norm and Normalized Directional Sharpness (NDS). We find that Muon and Adam have comparable update norms, so Muon's smaller curvature penalty is driven by lower NDS, not update scale. Third, we study how training data and model structure shape Muon's NDS advantage. Using Zipf-Probabilistic Context-Free Grammar (PCFG) data with controlled imbalance, we show that data imbalance amplifies Muon's NDS advantage over Adam. A within-/cross-layer decomposition further shows that, in the middle and late stages of training, Muon's lower NDS is mainly sustained by smaller within-layer curvature. Beyond empirical evidence, we analyze stylized quadratic problems with heterogeneous curvature and gradient alignment toward high-curvature modes. We prove that Muon attains a smaller average NDS than GD by balancing update energy across curvature groups; when curvature heterogeneity is sufficiently strong, this also yields lower local quadratic loss after the same number of steps.

</details>


### [169] [Towards Accurate Model Selection in Deep Unsupervised Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04665)
*Kaichao You,Ximei Wang,Mingsheng Long,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Deep Embedded Validation (DEV)方法，通过将适应后的特征表示嵌入验证过程，实现无偏且低方差的目标风险估计，解决深度无监督域适应中模型选择标准缺失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度无监督域适应领域缺乏准确、标准化的模型选择方法，现有方法存在高偏差、受限、不稳定或争议性（需使用标注目标数据）等问题，阻碍了该领域的进一步发展。

Method: 提出Deep Embedded Validation (DEV)，将适应后的特征表示嵌入验证过程，获得目标风险的无偏估计且方差有界，并通过控制变量技术进一步降低方差。

Result: 理论上证明了DEV方法无偏性及方差有界性，实验上验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: DEV为深度无监督域适应提供了一种准确、标准化的模型选择方法，解决了该领域长期存在的评估难题。

Abstract: Deep unsupervised domain adaptation (Deep UDA) methods successfully leverage rich labeled data in a source domain to boost the performance on related but unlabeled data in a target domain. However, algorithm comparison is cumbersome in Deep UDA due to the absence of accurate and standardized model selection method, posing an obstacle to further advances in the field. Existing model selection methods for Deep UDA are either highly biased, restricted, unstable, or even controversial (requiring labeled target data). To this end, we propose \textit{Deep Embedded Validation} (\textbf{DEV}), which embeds adapted feature representation into the validation procedure to obtain unbiased estimation of the target risk with bounded variance. The variance is further reduced by the technique of control variate. The efficacy of the method has been justified both theoretically and empirically.

</details>


### [170] [Learning Long Range Spatio-Temporal Representations over Continuous Time Dynamic Graphs with State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04672)
*Ayushman Raghuvanshi,Thummaluru Siddartha Readdy,Sundeep Prabhakar Chepuri,Mahesh Chandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CTDG-SSM，一种基于状态空间模型的连续时间动态图框架，通过拓扑感知的HiPPO算子实现长程时空信息传播，在动态链路预测、节点分类和序列分类任务上达到SOTA，尤其在需要长程时空推理的数据集上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间动态图方法仅能捕获一阶或局部时间邻域信息，无法有效进行多跳/全局结构模式的长程信息传播，亟需一种能同时建模长期时间依赖和全局拓扑结构的框架。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，首先提出连续时间拓扑感知高阶多项式投影算子（CTT-HiPPO），通过将经典HiPPO解投影到拉普拉斯矩阵的多项式上，实现拓扑感知的记忆更新；进而推导出等价的连续时间动态图状态空间模型（CTDG-SSM），并采用零阶保持法获得计算高效的离散形式。

Result: 在动态链路预测、动态节点分类和序列分类多个基准上达到SOTA，尤其在需要长程时间推理和空间推理的数据集上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: CTDG-SSM通过拓扑感知的状态空间建模有效解决了CTDG中的长程信息传播瓶颈，为动态图表示学习提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: Continuous-time dynamic graphs (CTDGs) provide a richer framework to capture fine-grained temporal patterns in evolving relational data. Long-range information propagation is a key challenge while learning representations, wherein it is important to retain and update information over long temporal horizons. Existing approaches restrict models to capture one-hop or local temporal neighborhoods and fail to capture multi-hop or global structural patterns. To mitigate this, we derive a parameter-efficient state-space modeling framework for continuous-time dynamic graphs (CTDG-SSM) from first principles. We first introduce continuous-time Topology-Aware higher order polynomial projection operator (CTT-HiPPO), a novel memory-based reformulation of HiPPO to jointly encode temporal dynamics and graph structure. The solution from CTT-HiPPO is obtained by projecting the classical HiPPO solution through a polynomial of the Laplacian matrix, yielding topology-aware memory updates that admit an equivalent state-space formulation for CTDGs (CTDG-SSM). Then a computationally efficient discrete formulation is obtained using the zero-order hold approach for model implementation.
  Across benchmarks on dynamic link prediction, dynamic node classification, and sequence classification, CTDG-SSM achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, it achieves large performance gains on datasets that require long range temporal (LRT) and spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [171] [Test-Time Compute Scaling for ASR with Depth-Conditioned Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04678)
*Yacouba Kaloga,Shashi Kumar,Shakeel A. Sheikh,Driss Khalil,Petr Motlicek,Ina Kodrasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LARM是一种深度条件循环Transformer，通过可控制的测试时计算扩展，实现非自回归语音识别的性能提升，在LibriSpeech上达到与更深层非共享参数模型竞争的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端ASR系统在推理时使用固定深度声学编码器，难以在测试时通过额外计算换取更好识别效果，除非训练更大模型。

Method: 提出LARM（深度条件循环Transformer），包含：稀疏CTC检查点、监督时钟嵌入、FiLM深度条件化、延迟软后验反馈。这些组件将循环结构化为识别检查点与潜在细化阶段，使共享权重在循环步骤间特化。

Result: 在LibriSpeech上，LARM的WER随推理循环次数增加而下降，性能与更深层非共享参数基线相当。

Conclusion: 测试时计算缩放可以扩展到非自回归连续语音识别领域，而不仅限于自回归语言模型推理。

Abstract: End-to-end ASR systems typically use fixed-depth acoustic encoders at inference, making it difficult to trade additional test-time computation for improved recognition without training a larger model. A natural approach is to reuse a shared Transformer block recurrently, but we find that naive looping does not fully exploit additional recurrent compute. We introduce LARM, a depth-conditioned looped Transformer that turns recurrent encoder depth into a controllable test-time compute axis. LARM combines sparse CTC checkpoints, supervision-clock embeddings, FiLM depth conditioning, and delayed soft-posterior feedback. These components structure the loop into recognition checkpoints separated by latent refinement phases and allow shared weights to specialize across recurrent steps. On LibriSpeech, LARM improves WER as the number of inference loops increases and achieves performance competitive with deeper unshared-parameter baselines. Our results show that test-time compute scaling can extend beyond autoregressive language-model reasoning to continuous non-autoregressive speech recognition.

</details>


### [172] [Cone-Compatible Monge Geometry for High-Dimensional Ordered Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04695)
*Lei Luo,Hongliang Zhang,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于闭凸锥诱导偏序的高维最优传输Monge结构，证明了在平方Mahalanobis成本下，锥与成本兼容的充要条件是锥在M-内积下是锐角锥。在此条件下，支持在锥链上的测度存在闭式分位数型最优耦合，形成锥链Wasserstein度量，为有序高维数据提供了可解释、方向有效的原始空间单调传输方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维最优传输因实数序与凸传输成本兼容而存在闭式解（单调重排），但高维情形通常没有闭式解。本文试图从偏序出发，在高维空间中恢复类似的Monge结构，使有序高维数据也能获得可解释的闭式最优传输。

Method: 引入锥兼容Monge几何：用闭凸锥K诱导偏序（y-x∈K当且仅当x⪯_K y），定义成本与偏序的兼容性（有序对满足Monge交换不等式）。对于平方Mahalanobis成本c_M(x,y)=(x-y)^⊤M(x-y)，证明兼容性等价于锥在M-内积下是锐角锥（∀u,v∈K, u^⊤Mv≥0），即K⊆K_M^*。在此条件下，对支持在锥链上的测度构造分位数型闭式最优耦合。

Result: 得到了锥-成本兼容性的尖锐刻画；建立了锥链Wasserstein度量及其与有向锥传输代价的区别；发展了可行性、对偶性、稳定性、逼近、高斯恢复、统计和计算等方面的理论结果。

Conclusion: 该方法不是通用的快速替代方案（如切片或树Wasserstein距离），而是为有序高维数据提供可解释、方向有效、保留原始空间的单调传输工具，与现有方法互补。

Abstract: High-dimensional optimal transport is seldom available in closed form. The one-dimensional case is exceptional because the order of the real line is compatible with convex transport costs, making monotone rearrangement optimal. This paper studies when an analogous Monge structure can be recovered in higher dimensions from a partial order. We introduce a cone-compatible Monge geometry: a closed convex cone (K) induces the order (x\preceq_K y) whenever (y-x\in K), and is compatible with a cost if ordered pairs satisfy a Monge exchange inequality. For squared Mahalanobis costs (c_M(x,y)=(x-y)^\top M(x-y)), we prove a sharp characterization: compatibility holds exactly when (K) is acute under the (M)-inner product, namely (u^\top Mv\ge0) for all (u,v\in K), equivalently (K\subseteq K_M^*). Under this condition, measures supported on cone chains admit a quantile-type closed-form optimal coupling, yielding exact transport under the original ground cost rather than after projection or metric replacement. We distinguish the resulting cone-chain Wasserstein metric on canonically ordered chain distributions from an extended directed cone transport cost on general measures, and develop feasibility, duality, stability, approximation, Gaussian recovery, statistical, and computational results. The theory is complementary to sliced and tree Wasserstein distances: it is not a universal fast surrogate, but a way to obtain interpretable, direction-valid, original-space monotone transport for ordered high-dimensional data.

</details>


### [173] [Graph-Guided Universum Learning in Generalized Eigenvalue Proximal SVMs for Alzheimer's Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04699)
*Yogesh Kumar,Vrushank Ahire,Mudasir Ganaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种图引导的Universum学习模型（UG-GEPSVM和IUG-GEPSVM），通过构建MCI样本的图结构（高斯相似度、最小生成树连通性和多跳传播）捕获几何信息，并将其作为Laplacian正则化项替代传统的独立Universum惩罚项，用于AD与CN的分类。在ADNI MRI数据上的实验表明，两种模型均优于现有方法，UG-GEPSVM达到最高平均AUC 88.07%，且在高噪声下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GEPSVM及其Universum变体将Universum样本视为独立点，忽略了样本间的几何关系，这限制了分类性能的提升。

Method: 1) 以MCI受试者作为Universum数据；2) 基于高斯相似度、最小生成树连通性和多跳传播构建Universum样本图；3) 导出Laplacian矩阵以捕获MCI样本的几何结构；4) 将Laplacian正则化项替代传统独立Universum惩罚项，UG-GEPSVM融入广义特征值公式，IUG-GEPSVM融入改进的标准特征值公式。

Result: 在ADNI MRI数据集上，采用ICA和PCA特征及五种噪声水平进行实验，两种模型均一致优于现有GEPSVM和Universum方法。UG-GEPSVM达到最高平均AUC 88.07%，且在噪声增加时性能稳定。统计检验证实了改进的显著性。

Conclusion: 图引导的Universum学习通过利用MCI样本的几何结构信息，显著提升了AD与CN的分类性能，为基于结构MRI的AD早期检测提供了更鲁棒有效的方法。

Abstract: Early and accurate detection of Alzheimer's disease (AD) is important for timely intervention and disease management. Generalized Eigenvalue Proximal Support Vector Machine (GEPSVM) and its Universum-based variants have shown promising results for AD classification. However, existing methods treat Universum samples as independent points and do not consider the geometric relationships among them. This paper proposes two graph-guided Universum learning models, namely UG-GEPSVM and IUG-GEPSVM, for AD versus cognitively normal (CN) classification using structural MRI data. In the proposed framework, mild cognitive impairment (MCI) subjects are used as Universum data to provide intermediate information between AD and CN classes. A graph is constructed over the Universum samples using Gaussian similarity, Minimum Spanning Tree connectivity, and multi-hop propagation. From this graph, a Laplacian matrix is derived that captures the geometric structure of the MCI samples. This Laplacian-based regularization is incorporated into the learning process in place of the conventional independent Universum penalty term. UG-GEPSVM integrates this regularization into the generalized eigenvalue formulation, while IUG-GEPSVM extends the numerically stable improved GEPSVM framework using a standard eigenvalue formulation. Experiments on ADNI MRI dataset variants using ICA- and PCA-based features at five different noise levels show that both proposed models consistently outperform existing GEPSVM and Universum-based methods. UG-GEPSVM achieves the highest average AUC of 88.07% and maintains stable performance under increasing noise levels. Statistical tests further confirm the significance of the observed improvements.

</details>


### [174] [Contrastive Learning and Correlation Clustering for Sequences of Network Telescope Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04733)
*Jannik Presberger,Alexander Männel,Maynard Koch,Thomas C. Schmidt,Matthias Wählisch,Bjoern Andres*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用对比学习（无需预训练和标注）对网络流记录序列进行嵌入，通过Transformer模型学习序列间的语义相似性，并基于相关性聚类实现对互联网扫描器的分类，实验证明该方法有效且泛化性良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解互联网扫描器的活动具有挑战性，通常需要识别源之间的关联关系，而语义标注数据稀缺。现有方法依赖人工标注，成本高且难以扩展。

Method: 提出一个Transformer模型，对最小预处理的网络流记录序列进行嵌入，使用对比学习进行训练；基于模型输出的相似度构建相关性聚类问题，并局部求解。

Result: 来自同一源头的序列之间的相似度显著高于不同源头的序列，且该性质可以泛化到未见过的序列和未见过的源头上；相关性聚类结果与扫描器标签一致。

Conclusion: 对比学习可以在无标注条件下有效学习网络流序列的语义关系，结合聚类可自动识别扫描器活动，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Understanding activities of Internet scanners is challenging; it often requires identifying relationships between sources, a task for which semantic annotations are scarce. This work investigates whether semantically meaningful pairwise relationships between sequences of network flow records can be estimated by contrastive learning, without pretraining and without annotations. To this end, we propose a transformer model that embeds minimally preprocessed sequences of network flow records and train it using contrastive learning. With the similarities obtained from this model, we state a correlation clustering problem and solve it locally. Experimentally, we show: Learned similarities are higher on average for sequences originating from the same source than for sequences originating from different sources, and this property generalizes to unseen sequences of unseen sources. Moreover, correlation clustering yields clusters consistent with scanner labels. The complete source code of the algorithms and for reproducing the experiments is publicly available.

</details>


### [175] [Trace-Mediated Peak Bias: Bridging Temporal Credit Assignment and Cognitive Heuristics in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04735)
*Viktor Veselý,Aleksandar Todorov,Erwan Escudie,Matthia Sabatelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现深度强化学习中的"迹介导峰值偏差"(TMPB)现象：在中间资格迹深度下，智能体非理性地偏好有高奖励峰值的轨迹而非高累积回报的轨迹。该机制解释了人类峰终定律的起源，并指出自适应优化器通过二阶矩归一化可缓解此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间信用分配与非线性函数逼近之间的交互作用尚未被充分理解，现有研究缺乏对两者耦合导致的系统性失败模式的识别和理论解释。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示TMPB的机制：资格迹将远端TD误差放大为"梯度冲击"，固定步长SGD无法归一化导致全局高估；自适应优化器通过二阶矩归一化缓解该病理现象。

Result: 识别出TMPB这一深度强化学习的系统性失败模式；证明了TMPB是峰终定律的机制性解释；表明自适应优化是理性价值估计的理论必要性。

Conclusion: 类人显著偏差可能从分布式系统中信用分配的数学约束自然涌现，自适应优化是理性价值估计的理论前提。

Abstract: Temporal credit assignment is central to both biological and artificial intelligence, yet its interaction with non-linear function approximation is poorly understood. We identify a systematic failure mode in deep reinforcement learning (RL) termed Trace-Mediated Peak Bias (TMPB). At intermediate eligibility trace depths, agents irrationally prefer trajectories with high-magnitude reward ``peaks'' over alternatives with higher cumulative returns. This provides a mechanistic account of the Peak-End Rule: a human memory bias where experiences are judged by their most intense moments rather than integrated utility. We show that TMPB emerges because traces amplify distal Temporal Difference errors into ``gradient shocks'' that fixed-step-size Stochastic Gradient Descent cannot normalize, leading to global overestimation. Conversely, adaptive optimizers mitigate this pathology via second-moment normalization. Our results suggest that human-like saliency distortions may emerge naturally from the mathematical constraints of credit assignment in distributed systems, and that adaptive optimization is a theoretical necessity for rational value estimation.

</details>


### [176] [Curvature-aware dynamic precision approach for physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04736)
*Yingjie Shao,Ioannis N. Athanasiadis,George van Voorn,Taniya Kapoor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种曲率感知精度控制器，在PINN训练中自适应切换单精度(FP32)和双精度(FP64)，利用L-BFGS优化器的曲率信息判断何时需要高精度，从而在保持预测精度的同时降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PINN实现中，单精度(FP32)计算效率高但易失败，双精度(FP64)稳健但计算成本高，二者存在效率和精度之间的权衡。需要一种自适应精度策略，在关键阶段使用高精度而其他阶段保持低精度，以兼顾计算效率与数值精度。

Method: 提出曲率感知精度控制器，复用L-BFGS优化器中的曲率信息来构建精度控制机制。当曲率信息表明低精度足够时保持FP32，当训练动态显示数值敏感或精度受限停滞时，提升至FP64进行计算。

Result: 在四个经典PINN失败模式基准和一个辐照驱动的常微分方程示例上进行了评估，并测试了不同神经网络架构。该方法在所有基准方程上均达到或略微超过全FP64的求解精度，同时相比全双精度训练减少了训练时间。

Conclusion: PINN优化中的精度敏感性具有阶段依赖性，仅在数值关键阶段选择性应用高精度可以在不牺牲预测精度的情况下降低计算成本。该方法有效缓解了PINN训练中精度与效率的权衡问题。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have become a promising framework for simulating partial differential equations (PDEs) by embedding physical laws directly into neural network training. However, recent studies show that PINN optimisation is sensitive to numerical precision. Existing implementations commonly use either single precision (FP32), which is computationally efficient but prone to failure modes, or double precision (FP64), which is robust but substantially expensive. This creates a trade-off between computational efficiency and numerical accuracy. To reduce the computational cost of double-precision training while retaining prediction accuracy, we propose a curvature-aware precision controller that adapts numerical precision during training rather than treating it as a fixed implementation choice. The proposed method reuses curvature information derived from the limited-memory BFGS (L-BFGS) optimiser to construct a precision controller, retaining FP32 when lower precision is sufficient and promoting computation to FP64 when the training dynamics indicate numerical sensitivity or precision-limited stagnation. We evaluate the proposed approach on four canonical PINN failure-mode benchmarks and an irradiance-driven ordinary differential equation example. We further test the proposed approach across different neural network architectures. The method consistently matches or even slightly exceeds full FP64 solution accuracy while reducing training time relative to full double-precision training on all benchmark equations. The obtained results indicate that precision sensitivity in PINN optimisation is phase-dependent, and that selectively applying higher precision only during numerically critical stages can lower computational cost without sacrificing predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [177] [Reproducing, Analyzing, and Detecting Reward Hacking in Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04923)
*Xuekang Wang,Zhuoyuan Hao,Shuo Hou,Hao Peng,Juanzi Li,Xiaozhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CHERRL是一个可控的奖励黑客环境，通过向LLM裁判注入已知偏见，稳定复现评分偏差，用于研究基于Rubric的强化学习中的奖励黑客现象及其检测与缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于Rubric的强化学习中，策略模型可能利用LLM裁判的潜在偏见进行奖励黑客，导致训练结果无效或不安全。由于这种黑客行为通常微妙且与多种裁判偏见纠缠，难以分析、检测和缓解。

Method: 提出CHERRL框架，通过向LLM裁判注入已知偏见，可控地复现奖励黑客现象。从可发现性和可利用性两个维度分析不同裁判偏见，并探索基于智能体的系统自动检测训练日志中的奖励黑客起始点。

Result: CHERRL能够稳定复现奖励黑客、显式观察奖励发散并精确识别黑客起始点，为研究奖励黑客机制和缓解方法提供了干净的实验测试平台。

Conclusion: CHERRL为基于Rubric的强化学习中的奖励黑客研究提供了可控实验环境，有助于理解不同裁判偏见的特性及自动检测奖励黑客的发生。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning (RL) uses an LLM-as-a-Judge (LaaJ) to score model outputs according to rubrics as rewards. However, policy models may exploit latent biases in the judge, leading to reward hacking and ineffective or unsafe training outcomes. In real-world rubric-based RL, such hacking behaviors are often subtle and entangled with multiple judge biases, making them difficult to analyze, detect, and mitigate. In this paper, we introduce CHERRL, a controllable hacking environment for rubric-based RL. By injecting known biases into LaaJ, CHERRL enables stable reproduction of reward hacking, explicit observation of reward divergence, and precise identification of hacking onset. This provides a clean experimental testbed for studying the mechanisms and mitigations of reward hacking in rubric-based RL. To demonstrate its utility, we analyze different judge biases from the perspectives of discoverability and exploitability, and explore an agent-based system for automatically detecting reward hacking onset from training logs. The code and environment are publicly available at https://github.com/THUAIS-Lab/CHERRL.

</details>


### [178] [An Empirical Audit of Input Encoders for Multi-Channel Signal Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04752)
*Ossi Lehtinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了八种将多通道标量信号编码为Transformer输入向量的方法，发现标准线性投影（nn.Linear(C, d_model)）在实际效果上与所有替代方案几乎等价，唯一的例外是共享标量基线和通道独立基线明显失败。推荐默认使用线性投影，仅在确有理由时才考虑更复杂的编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer处理多通道时序信号时，需要将每个时间步的C个通道值嵌入到一个d_model维向量中。现有研究对如何设计这一输入编码器缺乏系统性比较，不同论文采用不同编码方式，但各方案的相对优劣未得到明确审计。

Method: 在合成基准（通道身份信息具有信息量）和ETTh1真实数据上，以负对数似然为指标，对八种编码器进行审计：共享标量基线、逐通道线性投影、正交正则化、非线性MLP主干、分块拼接、通道独立架构、通道即token架构、投影位置编码。

Result: 标准线性投影与所有"第一梯队"替代方案的实际差距极小，统计上虽显著但实际差异不大。共享标量基线因信息论原因完全失败；通道独立（PatchTST风格）基线在两项基准上表现不佳，且在合成数据上普遍过拟合。投影位置编码在小通道数时略优，非线性MLP在最大通道数时略优。

Conclusion: 默认使用nn.Linear(C, d_model)即可，仅在任务确有必要时才采用更复杂的编码方案。实验代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Transformers consuming multi-channel scalar signals must embed $C$ simultaneous values into one $d_{\text{model}}$-dimensional vector per time step. We empirically audit eight input encoders -- spanning a shared-scalar baseline, per-channel linear projections, an orthogonality regulariser, a nonlinear MLP stem, block-partitioned concatenation, channel-independent and channel-as-token architectures, and a projected positional encoding -- on a synthetic benchmark designed to make channel identity informative and on ETTh1 as a real-data check, measured in next-step negative log-likelihood (NLL). The headline is one of practical near-equivalence within a wide "top tier": the standard per-channel linear projection (nn.Linear(C, $d_{\text{model}}$)) matches every alternative in that tier up to small, statistically real but practically modest, differences. Two encoders lose decisively: the shared-scalar baseline, which collapses for information-theoretic reasons we make explicit, and the channel-independent PatchTST-spirit baseline, which underperforms on both benchmarks and overfits universally on the synthetic one. Paired tests resolve two small gaps: projecting the sinusoidal positional encoding through a learned linear layer edges the rest at small $C$, with a direct geometric probe showing the mechanism is positional-channel orthogonalisation; a nonlinear MLP stem edges them at the largest $C$ we test, with the gap shrinking under more training data. The practical recommendation is to use nn.Linear(C, $d_{\text{model}}$) by default and reach for something more elaborate only when the task at hand gives a real reason to do so. Code and data to reproduce every experiment in this paper are available at https://github.com/OssiLehtinen/channel-encoder-audit

</details>


### [179] [Data Attribution in Large Language Models via Bidirectional Gradient Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04928)
*Frédéric Berdoz,Luca A. Lanzendörfer,Kaan Bayraktar,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双向梯度优化的训练数据归因方法（TDA），通过扰动基模型并测量训练样本上的损失变化，实现了对自回归LLM输出结果的数据级归因，在事实性和风格性归因上均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在广泛部署中引发了治理、问责和数据溯源的关键问题，而理解哪些训练数据最影响模型输出仍是一个根本性的开放问题。

Method: 基于逆向公式，通过在生成文本样本上进行双向梯度优化（梯度上升和下降）扰动基模型，并测量训练样本上的损失变化，支持任意数据粒度的归因。

Result: 在已知数据集的预训练模型上，该方法在影响指标上优于以往工作。

Conclusion: 该方法增强了模型可解释性，是实现可问责AI系统的必要前提。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across diverse applications, raising critical questions for governance, accountability, and data provenance. Understanding which training data most influenced a model's output remains a fundamental open problem. We address this challenge through training data attribution (TDA) for auto-regressive LLMs by expanding upon the inverse formulation: How would training data be affected if the model had seen the generated output during training? Our method perturbs the base model using bidirectional gradient optimization (gradient ascent and descent) on a generated text sample and measures the resulting change in loss across training samples. Our framework supports attribution at arbitrary data granularity, enabling both factual and stylistic attribution. We evaluate our method against baselines on pretrained models with known datasets, and show that it outperforms previous work on influence metrics, thereby enhancing model interpretability, an essential requirement for accountable AI systems.

</details>


### [180] [Beyond Structural Symmetries: Linear Mode Connectivity via Neuron Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04754)
*Vincent Bürgin,Daniel Herbst,Ya-Wei Eileen Lin,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有效函数类理论框架，研究参数对称性如何影响神经网络中的神经元可识别性、表示合并及损失景观，发现即使在结构不对称模型中仍存在大量近似等价解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中许多重要现象（如线性模式连通性、训练动态的结构化行为）与参数对称性密切相关，但参数、数据和表示之间的精确相互作用尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 建立了有效函数类的理论框架，形式化定义了神经元在其输入支持上可实现的函数集及其实现的范数代价；通过跨独立训练运行的神经元可识别性来形式化有效对称破缺。

Result: 1) 即使在结构不对称的模型中，神经网络也存在大量近似等价的解；2) 神经元可识别性使得无需预先对齐即可进行表示合并；3) 刻画了这种合并何时允许线性低损失路径的存在。

Conclusion: 有效函数类在影响损失景观中起着关键作用，为理解参数对称性、表示合并和优化动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: Many striking phenomena in deep learning, such as linear mode connectivity and the structured behavior of training dynamics, are closely tied to parameter symmetries: transformations that leave the realized function unchanged. Despite growing attention to parameter symmetries, the exact interplay between parameters, data, and representations remains underexplored. To investigate this, we develop a theoretical framework of effective function classes, i.e., the set of functions a neuron can realize on its input support, and the norm cost of realizing them. We then formalize effective symmetry breaking via neuron identifiability across independent training runs. Our analysis shows that neural networks can admit large families of approximately equivalent solutions even in structurally asymmetric models. We further show that neuron identifiability enables representation merging without prior alignment, and characterize when such merging admits a linear low-loss path. These findings highlight the role of effective function classes in affecting the loss landscape.

</details>


### [181] [Validity Threats for Foundation Model Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05029)
*Gunnar König,Martin Pawelczyk,Ulrike von Luxburg,Sebastian Bordt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了在大模型研究中，受限于计算预算的实验近似策略（代理实验、观察性研究、单次运行设计）虽然节约了计算成本，但引入了不同类型的有效性威胁（统计、内部、外部、构念效度）。作者提出将基础模型研究视为因果推断问题，并借鉴社会科学四类效度框架评估不同策略，发现每种策略都有其独特的效度特征曲线。该框架为研究者提供了审视研究设计有效性威胁的实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受控实验是机器学习研究的基石，但在现代基础模型的规模下已变得极其昂贵。学界转而使用代理实验、观察性研究和单次运行设计等低成本近似策略，但这些策略的有效性和潜在缺陷尚未被系统性地审视。

Method: 将基础模型研究视为因果推断问题，借鉴实证社会科学中的四类效度（统计效度、内部效度、外部效度、构念效度）构建评估框架，系统分析不同近似实验策略的效度特征。

Result: 代理实验以牺牲外部效度和构念效度换取统计效度和内部效度；观察性研究面临混杂和效应异质性挑战；单次运行设计受限于处理单元间的干扰。揭示了若干在文献中尚未得到充分关注的有效性威胁。

Conclusion: 在大规模实验近似中不存在免费午餐，计算成本的节省必然以有效性威胁为代价。提出的评估框架为基础模型研究者提供了审视研究设计效度威胁的实用工具包。

Abstract: Controlled experiments are the backbone of machine learning research, but at the scale of modern foundation models, they have become prohibitively expensive. Instead, the community increasingly relies on research strategies that approximate the ideal experiment at a fraction of the cost: proxy experiments and scaling laws, observational studies with publicly available models, and single-run designs that leverage variation within individual training runs. In this work, we argue that there is no free lunch when approximating large-scale experiments on a compute budget. Specifically, savings in compute come at the cost of validity threats -- hidden and sometimes untestable assumptions that, when violated, can invalidate research claims. To help navigate such threats, we propose an evaluation framework that casts foundation model research as a causal inference problem. Within this framework, we evaluate different research strategies through four types of validity adapted from the empirical social sciences -- statistical, internal, external, and construct validity. We find that each strategy comes with a characteristic validity profile: proxy experiments trade external and construct validity for statistical and internal validity; observational studies face confounding and effect heterogeneity; and single-run designs are strained by interference between treated units. This analysis reveals several validity threats that have received insufficient attention in the literature. Overall, our evaluation framework provides researchers with a practical toolkit for scrutinizing validity threats in foundation model research~designs.

</details>


### [182] [Measuring Model Robustness via Fisher Information: Spectral Bounds, Theoretical Guarantees, and Practical Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04767)
*Chong Zhang,Xiang Li,Jia Wang,Qiufeng Wang,Xiaobo Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fisher信息矩阵谱范数的攻击无关鲁棒性度量方法，从理论上推导了常见架构的谱界，并设计了高效估计算法，实验验证了该度量与对抗脆弱性的强相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度神经网络鲁棒性评估方法依赖特定攻击算法，缺乏可解释性，亟需一种攻击无关、原理清晰且可解释的鲁棒性度量指标。

Method: 基于Fisher信息矩阵的谱范数定义鲁棒性度量，理论上证明FIM等于输入Jacobian的方差，推导了VGG、ResNet、DenseNet和Transformer等常见架构的闭式谱界，设计了幂迭代和Hutchinson估计等高效算法以支持白盒和黑盒评估。

Result: 在CIFAR、ImageNet和医学图像等多个数据集上的大量实验表明，所提度量与对抗脆弱性之间存在强相关性。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为可解释的诊断工具，补充基于攻击的评估方法，为理解架构敏感性和设计更鲁棒的模型提供指导。

Abstract: The robustness of deep neural networks is crucial for safety-critical deployments, yet existing evaluation methods are often attack-dependent and lack interpretability. We propose a principled, attack-agnostic robustness metric based on the spectral norm of the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM), which quantifies the worst-case sensitivity of the model's output distribution to input perturbations. Theoretically, we establish that the FIM equals the variance of the input Jacobian and derive closed-form spectral bounds for common architectures, including VGG, ResNet, DenseNet, and Transformer, providing the first theoretical robustness ranking. To enable scalable evaluation, we develop efficient algorithms, including power iteration and Hutchinson-based estimation, that support both white-box and black-box settings. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, including CIFAR, ImageNet, and medical images, and across multiple architectures show a strong correlation between our metric and adversarial vulnerability. Our framework serves as an interpretable diagnostic tool that complements attack-based evaluations, offering insights into architectural sensitivity and guiding the design of more robust models. Code is available at: https://github.com/franz-chang/SRP/.

</details>


### [183] [In-Context Graphical Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05042)
*Zehua Cheng,Wei Dai,Jiahao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ICG-I，一种自回归图Transformer，通过模仿变量消除并使用张量训练压缩中间因子，结合Dirichlet输出层和加权共形预测，在离散图模型边际推断中实现了高精度和可扩展性，在标准实例和受挫自旋玻璃问题上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散图模型边际推断方法在精确性和可扩展性之间存在矛盾：精确算法对高树宽图不可行，迭代近似方法（如信念传播、变分方法）在受挫拓扑上缺乏收敛保证。作者认为这种二分法源于错误的归纳偏置——迭代方法放弃了使精确推断正确的序列消元结构。

Method: 提出自回归图Transformer ICG-I，核心思路是通过学习方式恢复变量消元的序列结构：使用张量训练（Tensor-Train）压缩中间因子以控制计算复杂度，采用Dirichlet输出层和加权共形预测（Weighted Conformal Prediction）提供校准的、分布自由的覆盖保证。理论上证明了TT压缩误差在自回归链中线性传播，Dirichlet-Multinomial损失是适当评分规则，WCP在估计密度比下保持覆盖且退化可量化。

Result: 在标准实例上MAE从最佳基线0.041降至0.020；在N=500受挫自旋玻璃（BP完全发散）上达到0.048 MAE，全面超越所有基线方法达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过恢复变量消元的序列归纳偏置，结合张量训练压缩和共形预测校准，ICG-I成功地同时实现了高精度、可扩展性和可靠的不确定性量化，弥合了精确推断与近似推断之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Marginal inference in discrete graphical models forces a choice between exactness and scalability: exact algorithms are intractable for high-treewidth graphs, while iterative approximations (Belief Propagation, variational methods) sacrifice convergence guarantees on frustrated topologies. We argue that this dichotomy stems from a mismatched inductive bias: iterative methods abandon the sequential elimination structure that makes exact inference correct. We introduce In-Context Graphical Inference (ICG-I), an autoregressive Graph Transformer that restores this structure by mimicking Variable Elimination with learned, Tensor- Train-compressed intermediate factors, paired with a Dirichlet output layer and Weighted Conformal Prediction for calibrated, distribution-free coverage guarantees under topological shift. We prove that TT compression errors propagate at most lincarly through the autoregressive chain, that the Dirichlet-Multinomial loss is a proper scoring rule, and that WCP maintains coverage with a quantifiable degradation under estimated density ratios. We conducted intensive experiments to evaluate ICG-I and achieved state-of-the-art performance across all benchmarks. ICG-I reduces MAE from 0.041 (best baseline) to 0.020 on standard instances and achieves 0.048 on N=500 frustrated spin glasses where BP diverges entirely.

</details>


### [184] [Activation Steering of Video Generation Models via Reduced-Order Linear Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04775)
*Jihoon Hong,Alice Chan,Qiyue Dai,Julian Skifstad,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LA-LQR框架，一种基于降阶最优控制的文本生成视频(T2V)激活干预方法，通过将推理建模为动力系统并求解闭环反馈控制，在低维潜空间中实现最小侵入性的概念引导与安全去偏，有效减少有害输出同时保持视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2V模型在大型网络数据上训练后可能生成不良内容，而现有的激活干预方法（如激活引导）存在粗粒度、非前瞻性的问题，容易导致过度引导和内容退化。需要一种更精确、最小侵入性的机制来减少有害输出而不牺牲视觉质量。

Method: 1) 将T2V推理过程建模为动力系统；2) 通过对比提示对将高维激活投影到低维任务相关子空间；3) 在该潜空间中估计局部线性动力学；4) 求解潜空间线性二次型调节器(LQR)问题，得到时间步和层特定的闭环反馈干预信号，以最优控制方式引导激活朝向期望特征设定点。

Result: 在概念引导和视频安全基准上，LA-LQR相比基线方法减少了不安全生成，同时保持了提示保真度和视觉质量。提供了潜空间设定点追踪与原始激活空间特征控制之间的理论界，并实证验证了降阶潜动力学的保真度。

Conclusion: LA-LQR作为最小侵入性T2V激活干预框架，通过降阶最优控制实现了精准、前瞻性的激活引导，在安全性和质量之间取得了更优的权衡，为T2V模型的可控生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) models trained on large-scale web data can generate undesired content, motivating interventions that reduce harmful outputs without sacrificing visual quality. Activation steering offers an attractive mechanistic alternative to finetuning and prompt filtering, but existing T2V steering methods remain limited, typically applying coarse, non-anticipative interventions that can lead to oversteering and content degradation. To close this gap, we propose Latent Activation Linear-Quadratic Regulator (LA-LQR), a reduced-order optimal control framework for minimally invasive T2V steering. LA-LQR formulates T2V inference as a dynamical system and computes closed-loop feedback interventions that steer activations toward desired feature setpoints while penalizing unnecessary perturbations. To make optimal control feasible for high-dimensional video activations, we project activations onto a low-dimensional, task-relevant subspace derived from contrastive prompt pairs, estimate local linear dynamics in this latent space, and solve a latent LQR problem to obtain timestep- and layer-specific steering signals. We provide theoretical bounds relating latent setpoint tracking to raw activation-space feature control, and empirically validate the fidelity of the reduced latent dynamics. On concept steering and video safety benchmarks, LA-LQR reduces unsafe generations relative to baselines, while preserving prompt fidelity and visual quality.

</details>


### [185] [Failed Reasoning Traces Tell You What Is Fixable (But Not by Reading Them)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05145)
*Nizar Islah,Istabrak Abbes,Irina Rish,Sarath Chandar,Eilif B. Muller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将推理失败轨迹从废弃数据转化为诊断对象：通过三个轨迹级特征（源于可用的测试时干预结构）从失败轨迹的分布特征（而非文本）中提取可恢复性结构，实现对失败类型的聚类、后训练方法的表征（准确率84.3±4.3%，比多数类基线高20%），以及无训练的路由规则，在Steerable-Hard子集上救援率提升12.2%。这些特征和规则可跨模型族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练语言模型在推理失败时，常见的做法是花费更多计算进行额外尝试，而失败的轨迹被丢弃。作者认为这丢弃了关键信号：一些失败源于采样运气差（更多采样可解决），另一些是结构性的（无论如何重采样都无法解决）。失败轨迹中编码了可恢复性结构——即哪些测试时干预能拯救该失败。

Method: 提出三个问题级轨迹特征，从失败rollout的分布特征（而非文本内容）提取，这些特征源于可用干预措施的结构。利用这些特征对失败进行聚类，表征不同后训练方法的失败地形图，并构建无训练的路由规则。

Result: 在表征后训练方法上达到84.3±4.3%准确率，比多数类基线高20%；在Steerable-Hard子集（重试不足且存在有界干预可达的失败）上，路由规则将救援率提升12.2%。特征和路由规则可跨两个模型族迁移。

Conclusion: 三个特征可将失败轨迹从废弃数据转化为诊断对象，支持测试时路由和后训练分析，无需训练时或权重空间访问。

Abstract: When post-trained language models fail on reasoning problems, the common test-time-scaling response is to spend more compute on additional attempts, and the failed traces play no further role. We argue this discards a crucial signal; some failures come from unlucky sampling, where more rollouts help, while others are structural and resist resampling regardless of budget. We propose that failed traces encode recoverability structure: the inference-time signature of which test-time interventions can rescue a given failure. Three problem-level trajectory features, derived from the structure of available interventions, recover this structure from the distributional signature of failed rollouts, not their text. They cluster failures into stable regimes, characterize the failure topography of different post-training methods ($84.3{\pm}4.3\%$ accuracy, $+20\%$ over a majority-class baseline), and support a training-free routing rule that lifts rescue by $+12.2\%$ on the deployment-relevant Steerable-Hard subset (failures where retry is insufficient and a bounded intervention is reachable). The features and the routing rule transfer across two cross-family probes. The same three features thus convert failed traces from discarded data into a diagnostic object, supporting test-time routing and post-training analysis without training-time or weight-space access.

</details>


### [186] [UniFair: A unified fair clustering approach based on separation and compactness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04777)
*Antonia Karra,Vasiliki Papanikou,Georgios Vardakas,Evaggelia Pitoura,Aristidis Likas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniFair是一个统一框架，联合优化分离公平性（保护群体远离决策边界）和社会公平性（减少组内聚类代价差异），通过梯度优化方法实现k-means和深度聚类的公平性，在表格和图像数据集上以较小的聚类损失增加显著降低了群体差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准聚类目标（如k-means）可能对不同人口群体产生不公平的处理，而现有公平聚类方法通常只优化单一公平性概念，且忽视了聚类代价与决策边界几何之间的相互作用。

Method: 提出UniFair统一框架，同时优化分离公平性和社会公平性；开发基于梯度的优化算法用于分离公平和统一的k-means目标；通过自编码器潜在空间施加相同标准将其扩展到深度聚类。

Result: 在表格和图像数据集上的实验表明，UniFair以仅适度增加聚类损失的代价，有效降低了边界相关和代价相关的群体差异。

Conclusion: UniFair通过联合优化两种公平性概念，在保持聚类质量的同时显著提升聚类的公平性，为公平聚类提供了更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: Clustering is increasingly used to support high-impact decisions, yet standard objectives such as $k$-means can produce clusterings that treat demographic groups unequally. Existing fair clustering methods typically optimize a single notion of fairness and often overlook how clustering costs interact with the geometry of the induced decision boundaries. We propose \textsc{UniFair}, a unified framework that jointly optimizes \emph{separation fairness} and \emph{social fairness}. Separation fairness encourages protected groups to lie farther from the induced decision boundaries, while social fairness reduces disparities in within-cluster distortion by penalizing group-wise clustering costs. We develop gradient-based optimization procedures for separation-fair and unified $k$-means objectives, and extend them to deep clustering by enforcing the same criteria in the latent space of an autoencoder. Experiments on tabular and image datasets show that \textsc{UniFair} reduces both boundary-related and cost-based group disparities with only a modest increase in clustering loss.

</details>


### [187] [Reinforcement Learning from Rich Feedback with Distributional DAgger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05152)
*Rishabh Agrawal,Jacob Fein-Ashley,Paria Rashidinejad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DistIL方法，通过分布化DAgger算法利用丰富反馈（执行轨迹、工具输出、专家修正等）进行前向交叉熵学习，相比传统RLVR仅依赖单一正确性比特，实现了单调策略改进和更好的Pass@N性能，在科学推理、编程和数学问题求解上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）仅使用单一比特（答案是否正确）作为奖励信号，忽略了执行轨迹、工具输出、专家修正和模型自评估等丰富的反馈信息，限制了学习效率和性能。

Method: 提出分布化的DAgger算法变体，学习器可以访问当前策略访问状态上的专家分布，通过前向交叉熵目标进行优化，该目标支持黑盒专家且能通过序列级梯度将未来专家-学生分歧反向传播至早期决策。

Result: 在科学推理、编程和数学问题求解等多个领域，DistIL方法优于RLVR和基于自蒸馏的RL基线。理论分析表明，基于反向KL或JS散度的自蒸馏目标无法保证单调策略改进（即使专家奖励更高，更新也可能增加错误动作概率），而前向交叉熵能实现单调策略改进并具有遗憾保证。

Conclusion: 前向交叉熵优化能够有效利用丰富的反馈信号，实现更优的策略学习，为超越传统RLVR范式的推理模型训练提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: Reasoning models have advanced rapidly, but the dominant reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) recipe remains surprisingly narrow: sample many responses and reward each with a single bit indicating whether the final answer is correct. Yet many settings provide rich feedback, including execution traces, tool outputs, expert corrections, and model self-evaluations. We study how to use such feedback through a distributional variant of the classic imitation learning algorithm DAgger, where the learner has local access to an expert distribution on states visited by the current policy. This yields a simple forward cross-entropy objective that admits a blackbox expert and whose sequence-level gradient {conduct rich credit assignment by propagating} future expert-student disagreement back to earlier decisions. We show that prior RL with self-distillation objectives based on reverse KL or Jensen-Shannon fail to guarantee monotonic policy improvement: even when the expert has higher reward, their updates may increase probability on worse actions. In contrast, we show that forward cross-entropy admits monotonic policy improvement and enjoys guarantees on regret. We further show that our objective optimizes a lower bound on teacher-weighted likelihood of success, leading to improved Pass@N. Empirically, our approach, DistIL, improves over RLVR and RL with self-distillation baselines across a variety of domains: scientific reasoning, coding, and solving hard mathematical problems.

</details>


### [188] [Uncertainty-Aware (Un)Supervised Few-Shot User Adaptation for On-Device Personalized Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04798)
*Maximilian Burzer,Till Riedel,Michael Beigl,Tobias Röddiger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需梯度的个人化框架，通过原型网络和闭式贝叶斯原型估计，在少量标记或未标记校准数据下，显著提升预训练HAR分类器对新用户的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于传感器的HAR模型在新用户上因个体运动模式和传感器放置的域偏移而性能下降，需要轻量、鲁棒且适应不同标签可用性的个人化方法。

Method: 将预训练HAR分类器重新用作原型网络，利用先验原型保持零样本性能并正则化适应；对标记校准数据采用闭式贝叶斯原型估计，对未标记数据扩展相同原理；适应过程仅需闭式原型更新，无需梯度计算。

Result: 在四个数据集上，仅需每类活动3秒校准数据（一次样本），监督适应提升macro-F1 +2.76~+33.44个百分点，无监督适应提升+0.56~+32.13个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架通过闭式原型更新实现高效且鲁棒的设备端个人化，适用于已有HAR分类器，兼顾零样本性能保持和少样本适应。

Abstract: Sensor-based Human Activity Recognition (HAR) models often degrade on unseen users due to domain shifts caused by individual movement patterns and sensor placement. Practical wearable HAR systems therefore require personalization methods that are lightweight, applicable whether calibration data is labeled, unlabeled, or unavailable, and robust under limited calibration. We present a gradient-free framework that repurposes pretrained HAR classifiers as Prototypical Networks using using prior prototypes, which preserve zero-shot performance and regularize adaptation. For labeled calibration, we introduce closed-form Bayesian prototype estimation and extend the same principle to unlabeled calibration. With only 3 seconds of calibration data per activity (one shot), supervised adaptation improves macro-F1 by +2.76 to +33.44 percentage points across four datasets, while unsupervised adaptation improves by +0.56 to +32.13 points. Since adaptation requires only closed-form prototype updates, the framework enables efficient and robust on-device personalization of preexisting HAR classifiers.

</details>


### [189] [STRIDE: Training Data Attribution via Sparse Recovery from Subset Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05165)
*Rishit Dagli,Abir Harrasse,Luke Zhang,Florent Draye,Amirali Abdullah,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STRIDE框架，通过在激活空间中学习轻量级"引导算子"来模拟训练数据的影响，将训练数据归因问题转化为稀疏恢复问题，实现比先前方法快13倍的LLM预训练归因，并在数据选择、数据污染检测等下游任务中验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于参数空间的梯度近似方法计算成本过高（数十亿参数），且依赖局部线性近似，难以高效准确地实现LLM训练数据归因。

Method: 将训练数据归因从参数空间转移到激活空间；学习轻量级"引导算子"（steering operators）模拟训练子集引起的模型行为变化；通过稀疏线性分解从算子对测试预测的扰动中恢复单个训练样本的影响力。

Result: 在LLM预训练归因任务上达到最先进水平，速度比先前方法快13倍；在数据选择、数据污染检测和定性分析等下游应用中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 激活空间中的稀疏恢复方法为LLM训练数据归因提供了一种高效、实用的替代方案，避免了参数空间方法的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Training Data Attribution (TDA) seeks to trace a model's predictions back to its training data. The gold standard for TDA relies on causal interventions, observing how a model changes when data is added or removed, but repeated retraining is computationally challenging for Large Language Models (LLMs). Consequently, most approaches approximate this effect in the parameter space using gradients. However, tracking gradients across billions of parameters is not only prohibitively expensive but relies on local approximations. In this work, we propose a shift: rather than estimating parameter changes, we model the functional effect of training data in the activation space. We introduce STRIDE (Steering-based Training Data Influence Decomposition), a framework that formulates TDA as a sparse recovery problem in the spirit of compressive sensing. STRIDE learns lightweight "steering operators" that mimic the behavioral shift caused by training on data subsets. By measuring how these operators perturb test predictions, we recover individual training example influences via sparse linear decomposition. STRIDE achieves state-of-the-art for LLM pre-training attribution while being an order of magnitude ($13\times$) faster than previous art. We further validate its practical utility through downstream applications including data selection, data contamination, and qualitative analysis.

</details>


### [190] [The Right Measure for Physics-Constrained Generation: A Co-Area Correction for Posterior-Consistent PDE Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04804)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，在PDE逆问题中，利用扩散模型和流匹配模型通过硬约束（投影或引导）进行后验采样存在根本性错误——由于Borel-Kolmogorov悖论，条件化一个生成先验在零测度流形上会产生偏差，遗漏了关键的co-area（Fixman）雅可比因子。作者提出CoCoS（Co-area Constrained Sampler），一种度量感知的约束采样方法，能够正确恢复后验分布，实验表明现有方法的偏差可达采样噪声底数的20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前广泛使用的基于硬约束的生成模型（扩散/流匹配）在PDE逆问题后验采样中的系统性偏差——它们因忽略Borel-Kolmogorov悖论和co-area雅可比因子而采样了错误分布，需要为不确定性感知的科学推理提供正确的修正方法。

Method: 理论分析上，作者利用Borel-Kolmogorov悖论和Fixman（co-area）雅可比因子证明了投影法和引导法在条件化零测度流形时的偏差来源；方法上，提出了CoCoS（度量感知约束采样器），通过纳入[det(JJ^T)]^{-1/2}因子来校正后验分布，使其与真实后验匹配。

Result: 实验验证表明：忽略co-area因子会导致后验误差达到采样噪声底数的20倍；最小位移投影（如PCFM）偏差为9倍；简单的标量重加权无法解决；而CoCoS能够匹配到金标准后验分布，误差仅在采样噪声范围内。

Conclusion: "满足物理约束"不等于"采样后验分布"；硬约束PDE条件化必须考虑co-area雅可比因子才能得到正确的贝叶斯后验；CoCoS为不确定性感知的科学推理提供了原则性的修正方案。

Abstract: Generative models -- diffusion and flow matching -- are increasingly used to solve partial differential equation (PDE) inverse problems, enforcing the governing physics as a \emph{hard constraint} (via projection or guidance) and reporting the resulting samples as a Bayesian posterior with calibrated uncertainty. We show that this widely adopted recipe samples the wrong distribution. Conditioning a generative prior on a hard PDE constraint is conditioning on a measure-zero manifold -- an operation that is intrinsically ambiguous (the Borel--Kolmogorov paradox) and whose physically correct resolution, the small-residual-noise limit, carries a co-area (Fixman) Jacobian factor $[det(JJ^{\top})]^{-1/2}$ that projection- and guidance-based methods silently omit. We make the bias precise, show that it grows with the heterogeneity of the constraint sensitivity, and validate it on controlled problems against an \emph{i.i.d.} ground-truth arbiter. The omitted factor is not a second-order detail: removing it inflates the posterior error to $20\times$ the sampling-noise floor; minimal-displacement projection (as in PCFM) is biased at $9\times$ the floor; and a naive scalar reweighting does not fix it. We introduce \textbf{CoCoS}, a measure-aware constrained sampler that targets the correct co-area posterior, and show that it matches the gold-standard posterior to within sampling noise. Our results imply that ``satisfying the physics'' is not the same as ``sampling the posterior,'' and give a principled correction for uncertainty-aware scientific inference.

</details>


### [191] [Scenario Generation for Risk-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Probably Approximately Safe Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04812)
